## Expedition 01 — Reference Environment & Gameplay Entry Baseline

Date: 2026-10-26 (Asia/Bangkok). Owner: Work 1. Layer A.

**Objective:** Determine which build/environment/entry claims can be established from the supplied archive and available execution environment. Establish a reproducible baseline or explicitly document why original execution cannot be reproduced.

**Scope/non-goals:** Static input identity, environment inventory, proposed control/stress cases and capture checkpoints. No gameplay mechanics, UI reconstruction, skin/rendering work, proprietary code copying or reference-asset publication.

**Prior record/repository:** `https://github.com/thgillwtnorizoh/ohno`; GitHub get_repo returned ID 1431318446, default `main`, size 0. Contents API returned 404 with “This repository is empty.” `git ls-remote` returned no refs; local clone showed no commits. No existing `re`, contexts, expedition marks, AGENTS.md or Work 2 components were available. Supplied contract was read completely and copied byte-for-byte to `re/Instruction.md`. No history was edited. Dedicated research publication is intended; final handoff identifies actual verified remote revision. No remote outcome is assumed here.

All 16 self-declared MD5s agree; certificate directory exists. UNRESOLVED dated official build and clean/modified status. No signature trust or independent official hash comparison was performed. Filename/module role assumptions were not used as runtime evidence.

**Environment:** Available host is Linux x86_64, kernel 6.18.55, no Wine/dotnet/PowerShell/display/GPU device. ffmpeg available but no reference capture source established. See `host-capabilities.json`. Reference Windows version/GPU/driver/resolution/DPI/refresh/window mode/settings are UNRESOLVED. Config in archive contains updater values only. No runtime execution/capture occurred.

Expected SHA-256 map `dc4112acfcb0e7f345c6350a0cf1640d74f7acdc6eb2ae77047023b09e330541`; WAV `c263760de082d12fd4a983296b361d1c29c0567f354b01a44149fe24e9609977`. ## Expedition 02 — Static Gameplay Initialization & Runtime Call-Graph Recovery

Date: 2026-10-26 (Asia/Bangkok). Owner: Work 1, exclusive reverse-engineering agent under the latest assignment. Scope: static managed control flow; no reconstructed runtime, renderer or gameplay implementation, including temporary prototypes. No changes inside `reconstructed/`.

**Question:** What bootstrap/mode-transition/Play-initialization relationships can be established from the supplied assemblies without original execution? Competing explanations: gameplay-DLL logic versus main-EXE logic; immediate construction versus request/commit separation; one Play constructor versus conditional variants; threshold-crossing callbacks versus threshold-tested callbacks. Smallest discriminating experiment: inspect metadata ownership, CIL tokens/calls, branch targets and ordered field accesses. Dynamic reachability remains a separate question.

**History and authority:** Read the entire supplied contract (identical to baseline `re/Instruction.md`), baseline contexts, environment report, mark and archive evidence. User explicitly selected historical commit `f0ddc9ec116acf67b4f3ed2436f0d0c6503fd31f`; GitHub verified its tree `23c307de9f2737453b69af4918566b24852bc4ab`. Current old branch head was the prior user-requested deletion commit `60346f73b28609456c8d69ae603b7b15edcae37e`. Created independent `work1/expedition-02-static` from the selected baseline; old branch/deletions untouched. Baseline files on the new branch remain unchanged and this entry is append-only. Current mission supersedes any reconstruction role in the older contract; no historical wording was edited and no exception to evidence/preservation requirements was used.

**Reference/environment:** ZIP SHA-256 `7089d49fe3e91be66e026b1d1a3245503f65580879b20bb81e7d97dd8534b738`; EXE SHA-256 `6e182c10d1813209d12753dbc70b3a5bba00fef4ecf64bc42051870e6dfe4b7d`. Same self-declared Stable40 / version-resource 1.3.3.8 baseline; exact official dated release, provenance and modification status UNRESOLVED. Static Python analysis on Linux; native original Windows, display, settings and capture unavailable. No game executed.

**Evidence:** `evidence/02/static-control-flow.json`, reproduced by `tools/static_control_flow.py` using dnfile 0.18.0, pefile 2024.8.26 and dncil 1.0.2. Inventory includes hashes/resource sizes; selected methods include owner/base tokens, RVA/file offsets, method-body hashes, virtual flags, signatures and syntactic cross-references. Offsets exclude CIL headers. Call/field arrays are `[offset, opcode, target]`. Direct call, callvirt, constructor and delegate-address sites are distinguished; none automatically proves runtime reachability. Local `--inspect-token` supports rechecking branch arithmetic without publishing original disassembly. Private exploratory graph/disassembly are not repository deliverables.

**CONFIRMED static findings:** gameplay/ui/seasonal DLLs each contain two types/four resource-store methods; osu!auth.dll has no recognised CLR header. Main EXE has 2,907 types/21,398 methods. Entry `06002c49` calls `060023b9`; conditional launch reaches host constructor `06004668`. Request `060046ad` writes pending mode separately from commit `060046b0`. OsuModes Play=2, Unknown=10. Commit's Play case has four priority-ordered constructors `060023f0`, `06000715`, `06004ab9`, `0600222e`; all special variants derive from base Play type `0200047b`. Full selectors and locations in the current mark. Common commit path registers active object before virtual initialization, then clears pending only if it still equals current. Positive cleanup parameter queues disposal rather than proving immediate cleanup. Constructor registers event callback `060046ef` via `060046b5`; handler calls commit(0). Host update `0600469e` calls transition-progress `060046a3`. That routine tests finite progress thresholds before updating: outgoing phase 0 uses min(100,p+rate*s), incoming phase 1 uses max(-1,p-rate*s); callback happens on the threshold-test branch, not immediately after arithmetic crossing. Static call graph has three direct commit callers.

**RECONSTRUCTED interpretations:** semantic aliases pending/current/previous/active and transition-progress roles follow field dataflow; presentation as a fade is not visually confirmed. **HYPOTHESIS:** source clock millisecond units and semantic names for special Play selectors. **UNRESOLVED:** full guard meanings, UI-to-request path, scheduler policy/threading/units, effective virtual targets for all paths, exact parsing/object/audio startup boundary, native/reflection-generated paths, runtime timing and equivalence. Enum names do not prove visible UI states; Play application mode is not a ruleset identification.

**Tests actually run:** whole EXE decode produced 19,659 nonzero-RVA bodies / 1,739 zero-RVA methods, zero decode errors, zero invalid instruction-boundary branch targets, 119,386 call-like sites. Evidence assertions via `python re/tools/check_static_control_flow.py re/evidence/02/static-control-flow.json` passed: pinned input, entry, enum, Play switch destination, four constructors, registration-before-initialization order and three direct commit callers. `python -m py_compile re/tools/static_control_flow.py re/tools/check_static_control_flow.py` passed. `--inspect-token 06002c49` yielded the expected call and return at code offsets 0/5; selected request/commit/progress bodies were inspected privately. Full analyser output regenerated twice and byte-compared. `git diff --check` and staged diff review performed before publication. Old records checked byte-identical to the selected baseline.

**Changed files:** this appended `re/contexts.md`; new `re/02-static-gameplay-initialization-and-control-flow.md`, `re/tools/static_control_flow.py`, `re/tools/check_static_control_flow.py`, `re/evidence/02/static-control-flow.json`. No binaries/assets/proprietary source, no changes to original archive, no changes to completed mark 01 or original contract. Publication revision and remote verification are reported in final handoff.

**Handoff/interface/validation:** current mark describes mode inputs, global state tokens, progress/rate/time dependencies, constructor selection and lifecycle hooks. Expected finite threshold, sentinel, constructor-priority and reentrancy cases are branch-derived reference predictions, NOT executed gameplay tests. No original differential, capture, import, Windows instrumentation or signature verification was run. Analysis-decoder completeness is not dynamic call-graph completeness.

**Next useful experiment:** instrument the pinned original Windows request, progress callback, commit, constructor and initialization boundaries for one control-map entry, logging selector fields, progress, pending/current before/after initialization, thread IDs and input times. Static continuation can trace semantic origins of the four Play selectors and map-load boundary from `06002237`. Work 2 must not turn unresolved selectors or guards into implementation assumptions.

## Expedition 03 — Mode-Request Guards and Redirect Semantics

Date: 2026-10-26 (Asia/Bangkok). Owner: Work 1, exclusive reverse-engineering agent. Objective: recover every explicit acceptance, suppression, normalization, redirect and direct side-effect site in original MethodDef `060046ad`. No runtime/gameplay reconstruction, replacement prototypes, Work 2 fixes or changes under `reconstructed/`.

**Preparation/provenance:** Read full `re/Instruction.md`, cumulative contexts and complete Expedition 02 mark. GitHub verified requested parent `586ed1d668d535533cd5822cf0fdd6790b61d5d0`, tree `f73cca9ee128d7a47d5ce88f3f1090b062882f17`; local reviewed index matched that tree. Created dedicated remote branch `work1/expedition-03-guards` from that commit. Prior marks, evidence, contract and tools unchanged; this entry is append-only. No authorised exception.

**Inputs/environment:** ZIP SHA-256 `7089d49fe3e91be66e026b1d1a3245503f65580879b20bb81e7d97dd8534b738`; EXE SHA-256 `6e182c10d1813209d12753dbc70b3a5bba00fef4ecf64bc42051870e6dfe4b7d`. Same self-declared Stable40/file-version 1.3.3.8 baseline. Exact dated official build and modification status remain UNRESOLVED. Static Python/dnfile/dncil analysis on Linux, no Windows execution/display/settings/capture. Original-game execution NOT performed.

**Competing explanations/experiment:** global-force boolean versus duplicate-only; pending Exit as first lock versus late suppression; two Menu redirects identical versus different flag behavior; suppressed duplicate as no-op versus pre-suppression calls; direct live callback iteration versus snapshot-first. Discriminating experiment: privately inspect original CIL and extract method identity, all branches, ordered token/constant sites, field signatures and exception regions. Predicates remain opaque token-labelled calls, not guessed login/license/network semantics.

**Evidence:** `re/evidence/03/request-guards.json`, extracted by new `re/tools/request_guard_evidence.py`; validator `re/tools/check_request_guards.py`. dnfile 0.18.0, pefile 2024.8.26, dncil 1.0.2. Target RVA 1c3098, file offset 1839768, header 12 bytes, code 750 bytes, total method body 792 bytes; SHA-256 `8e25430f79dd7f0cff8e522b79c12823a0e9b7371e3f1efd410017b94dce7c14`. Deferred callback `060046e6` SHA-256 `733f7e46430aa1895c2c3d71d9f28d75f4d8a4041723f4061175211ba25b95ac`. Evidence contains 63 blocks, 36 conditional/switch sites, 8 explicit returns and 2 finally regions. All offsets are code-relative; dncil EH offsets already exclude header. Branch skeleton deliberately separates finally unwinding in exception-region metadata; it is not a runtime/exception interpreter. Raw original bytes and disassembly not committed.

**CONFIRMED static findings:** Unknown normalizes to current and sets captured flag true; Menu bypasses signed-int gate, other effective modes can defer before any later guard. Deferred closure reinvokes whole request with captured arguments and live global guards. Edit-state check precedes Exit/Update exemptions and can reject them. Exit/Update skip two opaque predicates; failing other-target predicate chain redirects Menu with flag=true. When F2c48=true, SelectPlay redirects Menu without changing flag and bypasses Play-exit blockers; Exit/Rank/Busy also bypass those blockers. Other targets can be blocked by current Play, M3b0d false and F2a82 or optional F136d.F13bb==4. Same conditional group blocks Rank-to-Play for ordered F2c51<10000; bge.un permits NaN. M405e and optional M4060 occur before duplicate and pending-Exit suppression. Boolean only bypasses two duplicate checks. Pending Exit check is late and not bypassable by bool. Snapshot-selected item callbacks complete before pending write; two enumerators use finally disposal. Common helper calls/rate=10 precede optional Exit callback, exact rate=5/.6 selection, optional M3b2b call and F2a79=false. Phase=0 is last normal write. No local catch/rollback or direct commit call. No enum-range guard. Full token/offset table and precise short-circuit pseudocode in new mark.

**RECONSTRUCTED aliases:** pending/current modes, snapshot callbacks and transition rate/phase terminology based on field dataflow. **UNRESOLVED:** predicate/helper purposes and side effects, gate/rate/time units, selector product meanings, scheduler queue/thread semantics, actual reachable state combinations, natural reentrancy and exceptions. `flag=true` is not global force; missing semantics are retained as opaque calls, not implemented assumptions.

**Tests actually run:** `python re/tools/request_guard_evidence.py ../project_sources/01-osubackup.zip` twice produced byte-identical evidence; `python re/tools/check_request_guards.py re/evidence/03/request-guards.json` PASS for pinned input/body hash, CFG/returns, 34 explicit conditional mappings, switch, ordered side-effect sites and callback call. All branch destinations land on decoded instructions. Existing `static_control_flow.py --inspect-token` inspected 060046ad, 060046e6, 060046e5; metadata confirmed field/callee signatures. `python -m py_compile re/tools/request_guard_evidence.py re/tools/check_request_guards.py` PASS. Baseline file identity, contexts prefix preservation and diff whitespace checks PASS before publication. New mark contains 34 manually branch-derived reference predictions, NOT executed gameplay cases or an executable surrogate request function.

**Changes:** new `re/03-mode-request-guards-and-redirects.md`; new extractor/checker above; new evidence JSON; this contexts append. Earlier records unchanged. Publication is on dedicated branch; final handoff supplies verified remote commit. Original archive and user attachments unchanged. No reconstructed code or copyrighted reference assets published.

**Limitations/next experiment:** complete direct control structure recovered conditional on opaque call outcomes and normal return; transitive effects, exceptions and runtime reachability remain unresolved. On pinned Windows original, instrument request/deferred callback and predicates, logging arguments, field values, ordered calls and writes. Prioritize deferred Exit, flag-preserving SelectPlay redirect, Update blocked by Play-state gate, NaN threshold, pre-duplicate side effects and reentrant callback writes. Do not classify the published predictions as observed gameplay. Read current mark's interface/guard tables for concrete fields and validation cases G01–G34.

## Expedition 04 — Beatmap-to-Gameplay Object Initialization

Date: 2026-10-27 (Asia/Bangkok). Work 1, exclusive reverse-engineering agent. Objective: establish the smallest original-managed-code chain connecting an initialized Play component, selected map, reader, runtime circle/slider/spinner constructors and object registration. Non-goals: gameplay reconstruction, full slider duration/judgment/scoring, rendering algorithms, audio synchronization, application UI. No files under `reconstructed/` created or changed; Work 2 remains outside this investigation.

**Preparation/baseline:** Read complete Instruction.md, contexts, marks 01–03 and relevant baseline/selected-method evidence. GitHub verified starting commit `633cdd7605a1fd8c2d089e3ccdb14fafd6b29871`, tree `fa3e70e4afc8eb87ec9e1d8a2abec3466da96ea9`; local baseline matched. Dedicated branch `work1/expedition-04-beatmap` created from that exact remote commit. Earlier marks/tools/evidence unchanged and this journal entry appended. No authorised exception.

**Reference/environment:** ZIP SHA-256 `7089d49fe3e91be66e026b1d1a3245503f65580879b20bb81e7d97dd8534b738`; EXE SHA-256 `6e182c10d1813209d12753dbc70b3a5bba00fef4ecf64bc42051870e6dfe4b7d`. Same Stable40/file-version metadata 1.3.3.8 baseline; official dated build/provenance UNRESOLVED. Linux static Python analysis with dnfile 0.18.0, pefile 2024.8.26, dncil 1.0.2. Windows execution, settings, display/capture and real beatmap input remain unavailable. No original gameplay executed, imported or observed.

**Competing explanations/experiment:** map passed directly through Play constructor versus selected-reference capture in initialization; parsed-definition list later converted versus direct runtime factory calls during reading; one global factory versus ruleset-selected virtual factory; allocation as readiness versus queued completion/postprocessing. Read original CIL at each shortest-chain boundary, resolve metadata ancestry and reused virtual slots, inspect parser column/flag/constructor arguments and registration calls. No alternative implementation or beatmap-spec parser was used as proof.

**Evidence/tooling:** New `re/tools/beatmap_init_evidence.py`, `re/tools/check_beatmap_init.py`, `re/evidence/04/initialization.json`; new mark `re/04-beatmap-to-gameplay-initialization.md`. Evidence contains 66 selected MethodDefs, 32 owner/ancestor types, abstract/virtual flags, signatures, RVAs/file offsets/body hashes, token/constant/branch sites, exception regions and original FileSection/HitObjectType/PlayModes enum values. Offsets exclude CIL headers; EH offsets already code-relative. Raw original disassembly remained private, no proprietary source/assets committed. Tools collect/check evidence only and implement no gameplay objects or parser.

**CONFIRMED static chain:** M2237 calls selected getter M2c63 at 001 (reads F1a0f), stores map to Play.F13f8 at 006, then creates virtual work/completion delegates M223b/M223c via wrapper M0707 at 66e. Wrapper queues work, catches work exceptions and queues completion; thread policy/timing UNRESOLVED. Work M223b switches original PlayModes; Osu=0 constructs handler M1ecb. Handler base constructor M0a9c invokes virtual manager slot M0abb, Osu override M1ee1 constructs M20cd(true). Work refreshes map metadata via M1bc1 and configures shared handler state via M2258, then takes handler.F05f4 into Play.F138f. At 182 it invokes manager.M28a2(map,flags), which stores map in F17ee, flags in F17ef and factory from abstract slot M28bc into F17fa. Osu manager override M20d4 constructs factory M426d(manager). Work invokes M28f5(false,false) at 18f. Loader clears old state and conditionally calls M28f7(FileSection.All=511,true,false,false) when map.F0fcd=true.

**CONFIRMED static construction:** M28f7 calls map.M1c05 at 161 and StreamReader constructor at 166; M1c05 has direct filesystem and alternate-provider paths. Metadata FileSection.HitObjects=64. Reader type dispatch prioritizes bit1 Normal, bit2 Slider, bit8 Spinner, then bit128 Hold. It strips colour bits with -113, extracts colour-skip bits separately, bounds both x/y to 0..512 before integer conversion, optionally maps y to 384-y, and adds map.F100b to start/end values. Circle/slider/spinner interface calls at 164e/1aa4/1ba6 use M1870/M1871/M1872; those are abstract, not directly executable bodies. Ordinary Osu overrides M426e/M426f/M4270 construct runtime M20dc/M424e/M1d6d. Timing-point lookup at start+5 contributes a bool to circle/slider constructors. Concrete overrides forward only subsets of the richer parsed interface arguments. No separate parsed-definition object layer was demonstrated on this path.

**Initial properties/storage:** shared constructor M249b records manager F152f and visual collections. Circle M45ec writes position F252c/F152e, equal initial start/end F2523/F2524, Normal type flag1 plus NewCombo4. Slider M142d initially writes equal start/end, position, curve kind, repeat count via M1433, length F2528 and control-data lists; initial end is NOT final duration. Spinner M3201 uses center (256,192), separate supplied start/end, Spinner flag8 and compatibility NewCombo behavior for map version-like F0fd1<=8. Registration wrappers M28ef/M28f0/M28f1 invoke M28d2(object,false,true); it BinarySearches/Insert into F17fb, adjusts neighbor/visual/count state. Comparator/equal-time tie behavior UNRESOLVED. Object construction can allocate visual state, conditional on manager.F1800; no drawing rules were recovered.

**Remaining setup/variants:** M28f5 invokes M28e9 after parsing. Base body updates count and calls M28ea, which for F0fd1>5 invokes slider M144a(true) before further processing; older versions route differently. Slider duration equations/postprocessing are not recovered, though M144a contains end-time writes. Play work scans map bounds, initializes handler and performs more setup before wrapper completion. Completion failure differs from success; first visible/hittable update not established. All four Play types share links to base initialization, but M23f7 can return before it; M23f8 adds work around base; M071d/M4abe/M23fb override completion. Thus no equivalence among all Play variants or all rulesets is assumed.

**Confidence/interface:** static instruction relationships CONFIRMED; gameplay concept/field aliases RECONSTRUCTED; exact input reachability, stream-provider semantics, scalar helper edge cases, score/mod configuration purpose, final slider duration, complete flags, scheduler threads, first input/visibility/judgment UNRESOLVED. Current mark supplies inputs (map/flags/ruleset/row/version), outputs (factory target/type/start/end/position/registration/completion), original token tables and ten branch-derived expected-reference cases. Those cases have NOT been executed on the original game.

**Tests actually run:** extractor twice byte-identical; new evidence checker PASS for pinned EXE, 66-method coverage, selected map/dataflow, callback wiring, abstract/concrete slot matching, factories, constructor/registration/field sites. `python -m py_compile re/tools/beatmap_init_evidence.py re/tools/check_beatmap_init.py` PASS. Existing Expedition 02 and 03 evidence checkers both PASS. Six shared method body identities match Expedition 02 evidence. Branch destinations checked against decoded instruction boundaries. Baseline bytes, append-only contexts prefix, required report headings and diff whitespace validated before publication. No CLR/factory execution, dynamic maps, audio timing, image comparison or gameplay differential tests were performed.

**Next useful gameplay investigation:** narrow slider initialization/postprocessing M28ea→M144a to length/repeat/timing/velocity-to-end-time derivation, using one circle control and one slider when original Windows execution becomes available. This follows the verified construction boundary rather than implementing placeholder duration or first-hit behavior. Publication branch is dedicated; verified commit is provided in final handoff.

**Additional factory dependency:** circle/slider new-combo arguments are M2902() OR file NewCombo bit, with calls at parser offsets 1631/1a79. M2902 remains opaque; do not replace this argument with the file bit alone.

## Expedition 05 — Slider Duration, Timing & Postprocessing

Date: 2026-10-27 (Asia/Bangkok). Work 1, exclusive reverse-engineering agent. Narrow objective: recover original slider end-time establishment through M28ea/virtual M144a and necessary timing/length dependencies. Non-goals: full curve rendering, slider scoring/judgment, audio sync, caching or gameplay reconstruction. Work 2 remains suspended. No changes under `reconstructed/`; no proprietary assets/binaries/source redistributed.

**Preparation/provenance:** Read full contract, cumulative contexts, marks 01–04 and relevant evidence. GitHub verified requested parent `3b5e65fb38d9e70fafe03cc1820a49ba36a69939`, tree `d1826d4802ff361b3c20d797b12a8a35fe044952`; local baseline tree matches. Dedicated remote branch `work1/expedition-05-slider-timing` created from exactly that commit. Historical records/tools/evidence remain unchanged; this journal receives only this append. No authorised exception.

**Reference/environment:** ZIP SHA-256 `7089d49fe3e91be66e026b1d1a3245503f65580879b20bb81e7d97dd8534b738`; EXE SHA-256 `6e182c10d1813209d12753dbc70b3a5bba00fef4ecf64bc42051870e6dfe4b7d`. Contract attachment and repo copy retain SHA-256 `bf9952eec9d375a8e2fb661142390c2feff6570214f4ce09aa8063d6f34274f9`. Same Stable40/file-version metadata 1.3.3.8; dated official build/provenance UNRESOLVED. Current Linux host recheck: no wine/dotnet/DISPLAY or /dev/dri. Original Windows game/CLR execution, frame capture, user settings, real maps and Aspire inputs remain unavailable. No original execution occurred.

**Competing explanations / discriminating evidence:** generic M144a applies to all concrete sliders versus virtual subtype override; one direct length formula versus prepared-segment accumulation; raw-double versus integer-truncated timing selection; old formats skip timing versus interleave it with another pass; duration truncation versus absolute-end truncation; ticks change duration versus travel-rate algebraic cancellation. Inspected original CIL privately, resolved slot metadata and switch branch targets, traced assignments through timing helpers and final writer. No modern format documentation or alternative implementation was used as proof. Decoder scripts read metadata/CIL only.

**Correction of E04 interpretation (prior text preserved):** E04 says “Case 0 constructs handler M1ecb” and labels M1ecb→M20cd→M426f→M424e ordinary Osu. Actual M223b switch at code offset 00e has destinations [033,025,056,064] for Osu0/Taiko1/Catch2/Mania3. Thus M1ecb at027 is Taiko1. Osu0 chooses M0da8 or M113e; M0da8 derives from/calls M113e. Handler slot M1141 chooses special M3203 or ordinary M0651(true). Ordinary factory-provider M0652 constructs M48de; slider override M48e0 constructs M142d directly at017. The previously inspected T08c2/M424e overrides M144a with empty M4261, and manager M20d7 uses separate M4263 end computation. Constructor presence/order in the earlier checker did not prove switch-to-ruleset association. New checker explicitly guards this mapping. Formats<=5 call M28ec, which also invokes M144a(true), interleaved with older positioning work. Slider M142d stores raw supplied count in F0bd0 and effective R=max(1,count) in F2527, L in F2528 and equal initial start/end F2523/F2524. Manager M28be sets A=100*S/T into F0adf; S/T semantic aliases refer to map double F0b56/F0b57 (defaults1.4/1; identified metadata writes bound .4..3.6 and .5..8). Literal key-decoder output remains unexecuted; aliases are RECONSTRUCTED from use. M144a refreshes V in F0bfc via virtual M125b(start) unless F0be3. That abstract slot resolves to M28ab, which calls M1be3(start,true), yielding E; positive ordered E => (A*T)*(1000/E), otherwise A*T.

**Timing selection:** M1be3 scans actual list order, using conv.i4(point.time) converted back to double before <= query; last qualifying base/inherited index independently wins, defaults0. If inheritedIndex>baseIndex and its beat length is ordered negative, multiply base beat length by single clamped inverse-SV factor. Ordinary M0b78 clamps -value (single) to10..1000 then divides100; M0b79 upper10000 applies for stored map modes1/3. M1be6 instead compares raw double time and falls back to first item; M1be4 uses this for Q=A (format<8) or A/factor (>=8). Fractional offsets can therefore produce different selections for rate and Q. No mid-slider reselection of V in the base accumulation loop. M28ff parses timing data; M0b75 coerces a non-base point's ordered nonnegative beat length to-100.

**Length/duration/rounding:** M144a prepares segments, sums original single segment lengths into F0bdd, may replace L when zero or when F0be9 is active with geometric length or an opaque gated quantized length, and adjusts trailing segments using G-L and .0001 tolerance. It stores prepared segments/cumulative double lengths in F0bdc/F0bde when nonempty; count<1 returns before end store. For R alternating traversals, segment distance is conv.r4(cumulative difference); dt is conv.r8(1000f*distance)/V; add each dt to double accumulator initialized from start. Sole final base end store is conv.i4(accumulatedAbsoluteTime) at M144a:1378. Tick loops do not write that accumulator. Ideal finite projection Lprepared*R*E/(100*S) is RECONSTRUCTED algebra, not bit-exact implementation. CIL conversions and arithmetic order are preserved; x86 CLR/JIT excess precision and out-of-range conversion results UNRESOLVED. Normal parser numeric-validation bool is false; M09e4 then returns Double.Parse without its optional extra finite/range checks. With validation=true it checks ±limit/NaN. End overflow, malformed geometry, transitive exceptions and import success remain unresolved. Base M144a does not inherit another subtype M18e2's explicit >60000-duration rejection. **CONFIRMED static input/construction:** T03bb enum Linear=2; M144a switch0b4 entry2→07a2. **CONFIRMED static Linear algorithm:** M144a07a2–0877 loops consecutive point pairs, creates one interpolated segment per pair (inner count1), retaining single-category A+(B-A)*0/1 arithmetic. M110c stores endpoints F0a32/F0a33. Appended pieces get F0a35/F0a34=true; downstream flag meanings not assigned. M110d calls vector Length (single arithmetic, Math.Sqrt double, conv.r4); unadjusted sum converted to double stored F0bdd at08d0. Shared adjustment runs only when G ordered>0. Zero L or active F0be9 selects usual geometric replacement or opaque gated quantization from E05. Excess=G-L; trailing distance<=excess+.0001 or unordered removes that segment and subtracts its single distance. Otherwise equal endpoints exit without extension; unequal endpoints set end=start+Normalize(end-start)*(M110d()-conv.r4(excess)), then exit. Preserve conversions and the separate Distance versus Length calls.

**Boundary consequences:** positive requested remainder can disappear under tolerance; negative L on ordinary positive finite path can empty list; final coincident segment can block extension rather than using the prior direction. If all pieces zero, G=0 bypasses adjustment and zero pieces can remain. Source points are not rewritten by normal endpoint adjustment. F2528 requested/stored L need not equal the final cumulative length. Non-finite vector math, out-of-range CLR conversions, stale-state consequences and transitive resource failures remain UNRESOLVED, not convenient defaults.

**Storage/timing/loading:** M144a stores nonempty prepared segment list F0bdc at0ae1, allocates/clears F0bde and appends cumulative double sums of adjusted single lengths. Empty temporary result does not assign/clear those output fields; returns0bea before final end store. E05 time accumulation consumes final cumulative differences and writes end at1378, so duplicate-tail failed extension affects duration. Loading's M28eb/M28ec positioning can invoke slider translation M1457, which moves source/prepared endpoints without recomputing F0bde/end. Linear pair loop itself has no version guard; version5/6 affects manager processing order and7/8 affects shared Q dependency. Non-linear version branches are outside scope.

Further temporary presentation preparation exists, but its full subsystem/GPU/resource behavior is not reconstructed. Thus a start-ordered full list can have non-monotone ends for overlapping sliders, and the special BinarySearch cannot safely be replaced by a globally earliest-unexpired filter. Starting j=k, scan while j<Count-1, stop for start>upper, else increment; GetRange(k,1+j-k) includes the first beyond-bound object or untested final object. k==Count clears existing candidates; otherwise replaces with shallow range list. No refresh timer/previous-time gate in this method. Constructor allocates separate lists; M28da also removes from candidates; M28c5 full reset has no direct candidate-field Clear.

M46a6 draw-stage calls M28e5 at07d. It locks manager registry; Edit loops full list; other modes loop candidates, except Play with F13d8=false skips. Each F153a=true object gets virtual M24b5 at098. F153a's only managed direct writer is slider constructor M142d:180=true; fresh circle default remains false. Equality passes, separate time reads and unchecked int arithmetic retained. No mutation in either predicate.
